2026-10-17T08:41:43.1927459Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [profile] Dispatch Firmus prefill round-robin and cap decode at 31 per DP rank

Runtime-only change on the measured kernel-chain image e29dfde4...
(Actions 38939081379), compared against run 38945855851 (6521.98 good
output tok/s, concurrency 192, P3 TP4/DP4/EP4, D1 TP4/DP4/EP4). The
concurrency, pool, speculation, extra-slot count per rank and image are
unchanged so that the two measured factors below are the only
differences.

1. Prefill DP dispatch: total_requests -> round_robin. In the reference
   run the prefill DP ranks processed 5.9 / 5.8 / 4.5 seq/s (DP0 queued
   in 78% of seconds, DP3 in 31%). DP attention and MegaMoE step all
   four ranks together, so an underfed rank is lost prefill capacity,
   and the official workload has fixed-length requests, so equal counts
   are equal work. On ASPIRE the same change equalised the ranks
   (1066/1062/1084/1085 seqs) and run 39013218562 kept 8/8 greedy
   outputs identical to the total_requests run. The pinned SGLang
   registers the prefill DP rank for every method other than
   follow_bootstrap_room (disaggregation/common/conn.py), so decode still
   learns the true KV source rank. Decode keeps total_requests, which
   adapts to completions. The method is read per role from
   firmus-framework.json by the host side of sglang.sbatch; worker.sh no
   longer hardcodes it, and check-firmus-server-info.py verifies the
   effective value per role after the run.

2. Decode cap: 128 -> 124 (31 per DP rank), CUDA graph buckets end in 31
   instead of 32. Per-request ITL regressed on concurrent running N in
   the reference run gives ITL = 6.2 ms + 0.089 ms x N; at the old cap
   (N = 126-128) steady-state ITL p99 was 20.1-20.6 ms and 82 requests
   failed the 20 ms SLA. One fewer request per rank lowers ITL by about
   0.4 ms and keeps decode capacity at 124 / 3.4 s = 36 req/s, above the
   36-37 req/s prefill supply expected after change 1. SGLang pads each
   decode batch to the next captured graph bucket, so the cap must be a
   bucket: the 32 bucket is replaced by 31 (the reference run already
   executed its running-31 samples on the 32 graph). Extra slots go from
   16 to 17 so the request-slot pool stays 31 + 17 + 1 = 49 per rank,
   exactly as measured, and cap + extra x 4 = 192 = concurrency still
   lets every client hold a slot.

Expected from the plan note (research/runtime-performance/
2026-10-17-firmus-acceleration-plan.md): raw 7200-7400 tok/s, ITL
failures near zero, score 6950-7150; start-burst TTFT failures (114 in
the reference) are unchanged by this commit. Judge from the official
score, the per-rank prefill seq counts, decode running and the ITL
distribution; the [profile] pass after the normal benchmark gives the
kernel-level decode data for the kernel candidate run that follows.

Tests pin the new cap, bucket list, per-role dispatch flags and slot
arithmetic; the archived run-38369214512 fixture gains explicit per-role
dispatch keys (that run used total_requests for both roles). Shell
syntax, Ruff and the full test suite pass. First implementation by a
delegated agent; reviewed and adjusted (extra slots) before submission.
